- 反编译源码:~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/Terraria/(Chest.cs 的 SetupShop 全量段,先 grep -n "case" 定位各 NPC 段再精读)
- 本仓库实现:game/src/data/vanilla-shopstock.json + core/Game.ts 商店链(grep "shopstock\|shopStock\|openShop" 定位)

审计规则:
- 只读审计,禁止修改/创建任何文件,报告作为最终回复返回
- wiki 与实现冲突时以源码裁决;wiki 与源码不符要标注
- 每条结论给实现侧 file:line 证据(grep 到并读过;纯推断标 ❓)

必核清单(每 NPC:货品清单/解锁条件/困难模式换货/血月换货/月相换货/入住后刷新规则):
1. 全部 ~24 名城镇 NPC 的货品表逐条对账(重点:军火商按子弹进度换货、树妖按世界邪恶/进度、哥布林按月相/血月、巫医按 boss 进度+丛林住、蒸汽按环境、酒馆按 OOA 进度、公主全图鉴后)
2. 库存刷新:什么时机重掷(入住/每天/每次对话?)
3. 价格链消费:baseValue→折扣卡→幸福度(已知幸福度缺失,只确认价格链结构)
4. 商店 UI:购买数量(Shift 批量?)、售出路径是否存在(已知"卖给 NPC"路径缺失,确认)
5. 稀有物品概率售卖(派对女孩的稀有货/巫医的叶晶等)
6. 旅行商/骷髅商已审过(跳过,仅引用)

实现侧入口:node -e "const j=require('./game/src/data/vanilla-shopstock.json');console.log(Object.keys(j).length,JSON.stringify(Object.entries(j).slice(0,3)))" 看结构;grep -n "shopstock" game/src/core/Game.ts | head

9172	  /** 商店上架门（Chest.SetupShop if 门 → 本作状态映射；未实现事件门不上架） */
9173	  private shopCondOk(cond: string | null): boolean {
9174	    if (!cond) return true;
9175	    // 复合门（extract-shops.mjs parseGate：嵌套 if 的全部开门按 '&&' 连接，
9176	    // 如 `hardMode && ZoneGraveyard`——外层与内层同时成立才上架）
9177	    if (cond.includes('&&')) return cond.split('&&').every((c) => this.shopCondOk(c));
9178	    const f = this.world.flags;
9179	    switch (cond) {
9180	      case 'hardMode': return !!f.hardMode;
9181	      case 'downedBoss1': return !!(f['downed_4'] || f.downedEyeOfCthulhu);
9182	      case 'downedBoss2': return !!(f['downed_13'] || f['downed_266']);
9183	      case 'downedBoss3': return !!(f['downed_35'] || f.downedSkeletron);
9184	      case 'downedMechBossAny':
9185	        return !!(f['downed_125'] || f['downed_126'] || f['downed_127'] || f['downed_134']);
9186	      // NPC.downedPlantBoss（世纪之花商店门：爆破专家地雷 937 / 松露自动锤 1551 /
9187	      // 巫医 Tiki 套·灵液瓶·叶翼 / 画家回声涂层 5344 / 动物学家泥芽 4701）
9188	      case 'downedPlantBoss': return !!f['downed_262'];
9189	      case 'downedPirates': return !!f.downedPirates;
9190	      case 'moonPhase':
9191	        // 月相门：extract-shops.mjs 未捕获 case 相位值——恒 false，分档上架由
9192	        // shopStockFor 的相位实表负责（改 true 会全相位重复上架）
9193	        return false;
9194	      case 'night': return !this.world.clock.isDay;
9195	      case 'day': return this.world.clock.isDay;
9196	      case 'zoneSnow': return !!this.scene?.zoneSnow;
9197	      case 'zoneJungle': return !!this.scene?.zoneJungle;
9198	      case 'zoneGraveyard': return !!this.scene?.zoneGraveyard;
9199	      case 'zoneMeteor': return !!this.scene?.zoneMeteor;
9200	      case 'zoneUnderworld': return !!this.scene?.zoneUnderworldHeight;
9201	      case 'crimson': return this.world.crimson;
9202	      case 'bloodMoon': return this.world.clock.bloodMoon;
9203	      case 'xMas': return this.world.clock.xMas;
9204	      case 'eclipse': return !!this.world.clock.eclipse; // 日食（电子人 774 等）
9205	      case 'party': return partyIsUp(this.partyState);   // 派对进行中（派对商品族）
9206	      case 'lanternNight': return LanternNight.lanternsUp; // 灯笼夜（派对女孩 4702 放飞灯笼）
9207	      default: return false;
9208	    }
9209	  }

9217	      bloodMoon: !!w.clock.bloodMoon,
9218	      pumpkinMoon: w.moonEvent.kind === MOON_KIND_PUMPKIN,
9219	      snowMoon: w.moonEvent.kind === MOON_KIND_FROST,
9220	      invasionType: w.invasionType,
9221	      moonLordCountdown: w.lunarEvent.moonLordCountdown,
9222	      spawnMeteor: this.meteorPending,
9223	      downedMoonlord: !!w.flags['downed_398'],
9224	      // BossIsActive（:68-79）：boss 或 13-15（世吞体节/克脑爬行者）——本仓 Boss 单槽
9225	      // + 入场怪近似为「Boss 槽在场或世吞体节/爬行者在场」
9226	      bossActive: () => {

9253	  /** ShopHelper.ProcessMood 输入装配（:99-180——同屋/村扫描 + zone 快照 + 危险群系） */
9254	  private computeShopHappiness(npc: TownNPC) {
9255	    const w = this.world;
9256	    const home = npc.home;
9257	    const cx = Math.floor(npc.cx / TILE), cy = Math.floor(npc.cy / TILE);
9258	    // 同屋（<25）/村（25-120）计数（:295-332：以 home 为锚；不可同住门近似=全部计入）
9259	    let inHouse = 0, inVillage = 0;
9260	    for (const n of this.entities.npcs) {
9261	      const tn = n as TownNPC;
9262	      if (!(tn instanceof TownNPC) || tn === npc || tn.dead) continue;
9263	      const anchor = home ?? { x: cx, y: cy };
9264	      const tnAnchor = tn.home ?? { x: Math.floor(tn.cx / TILE), y: Math.floor(tn.cy / TILE) };
9265	      const d = Math.hypot(tnAnchor.x - anchor.x, tnAnchor.y - anchor.y);
9266	      if (d < 25) inHouse++;
9267	      else if (d < 120) inVillage++;
9268	    }
9269	    const sc = this.scene;
9270	    const zones: BiomeZones = {
9271	      forest: !!(sc && !sc.zoneCorrupt && !sc.zoneCrimson && !sc.zoneHallow && !sc.zoneSnow
9272	        && !sc.zoneJungle && !sc.zoneDesert && !sc.zoneGlowshroom && !sc.zoneBeach
9273	        && !sc.zoneDungeon && !sc.zoneRockLayerHeight),
9274	      ocean: !!sc?.zoneBeach, snow: !!sc?.zoneSnow, desert: !!sc?.zoneDesert,
9275	      jungle: !!sc?.zoneJungle, underground: !!sc?.zoneDirtLayerHeight || !!sc?.zoneRockLayerHeight,
9276	      hallow: !!sc?.zoneHallow, mushroom: !!sc?.zoneGlowshroom,
9277	    };
9278	    return computeHappiness({
9279	      npcVanillaId: npc.vanillaId,
9280	      homeless: !home,
9281	      distFromHome: home ? Math.hypot(cx - home.x, cy - home.y) : 0,
9282	      playerInCorrupt: !!sc?.zoneCorrupt, playerInCrimson: !!sc?.zoneCrimson, playerInDungeon: !!sc?.zoneDungeon,
9283	      zones,
9284	      npcsInHouse: inHouse, npcsInVillage: inVillage,
9285	      princessNearby: this.entities.npcs.some((n) => (n as TownNPC).vanillaId === 663 && !(n as TownNPC).dead),
9286	      isPrincess: npc.vanillaId === 663,
9287	    });
9288	  }
9289	
9290	  /** 当前对话 NPC 的快乐度价格乘子（ShopHelper.GetShoppingSettings——缓存到开店时） */

9292	
9293	  /** 商店购买:扣币 + 入包 + 刷新商店面板(可购态/余额) */
9294	  npcShopBuy(vanillaId: number): void {
9295	    const list = this.dialogNpc ? this.shopStockFor(this.dialogNpc) : [];
9296	    const entry = list.find(([id]) => id === vanillaId);
9297	    if (!entry) return;
9298	    // 折扣卡（discountAvailable：PriceAdjustment 0.8，Main.cs:41028 同源）× 快乐度乘子
9299	    const price = Math.max(1, Math.round(entry[1] * (this.player.equipStats.discount ? 0.8 : 1) * this.shopHappinessMul));
9300	    if (this.coinsOwned() < price || !this.spendCopper(price)) {
9301	      this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.NPC.NotEnoughCoins'));
9302	      this.openNpcShop();
9303	      return;
9304	    }
9305	    const key = VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[vanillaId];
9306	    if (key && ITEM_BY_KEY[key] !== undefined) this.player.inv.add(ITEM_BY_KEY[key], 1);
9307	    this.sfx.play('coin');
9308	    this.cb.onInventoryChanged();
9309	    this.openNpcShop();
9310	  }
9311	
9312	  /** 骷髅商离屏计时（CheckActive timeLeft=750 语义） */
9313	  private skeletonMerchantDespawnT = 0;

9315	  private updateTravellingMerchant(): void {
9316	    const w = this.world;
9317	    const t = w.clock.timeOfDay;
9318	    // 骷髅商：离屏累计 750 tick 消失（在屏重置——CheckActive 屏内矩形语义；
9319	    // 入侵不影响其消散）
9320	    const sm = this.entities.npcs.find(
9321	      (n) => n instanceof TownNPC && !n.dead && n.npcKey === 'skeleton_merchant') as TownNPC | undefined;
9322	    if (sm) {
9323	      const ds = Math.max(Math.abs(sm.cx - this.player.cx), Math.abs(sm.cy - this.player.cy));
9324	      if (ds > TILE * 62) {
9325	        if (++this.skeletonMerchantDespawnT >= 750) { sm.dead = true; this.skeletonMerchantDespawnT = 0; }
9326	      } else this.skeletonMerchantDespawnT = 0;
9327	    }
9328	    // 入侵/月事件进行中旅行商不到访（SpawnTravelNPC :5189 门；月事件原版经 !dayTime
9329	    // 间接压制——此处同入驻轮显式补）
9330	    if (invasionActive(w) || moonEventActive(w)) return;
9331	    const tm = this.entities.npcs.find(
9332	      (n) => n instanceof TownNPC && !n.dead && n.npcKey === 'travelling_merchant') as TownNPC | undefined;
9333	    if (tm) {
9334	      // 离场：白天过 48600 或入夜，且玩家离屏后移除（UnspawnTravelNPC）。
9335	      // 48600/54000 = 0.9（昼内进度，Main.cs:64477——曾误按 0.8 = 43200 提前一小时赶人）
9336	      if (!w.clock.isDay || (t - Clock.DAWN) / 0.625 > 0.9) {
9337	        const d = Math.hypot(tm.cx - this.player.cx, tm.cy - this.player.cy);
9338	        if (d > TILE * 62) {
9339	          tm.dead = true;
9340	          this.newText(Lang.text('LegacyMisc.35', Lang.npcName(368) ?? ''), 50, 125, 255);
9341	        }
9342	      }
9343	      return;
9344	    }
9345	    // 到访 roll 门（Main.cs:64483-64484）：!IsFastForwardingTime()（晷快进中不刷）&& 白天上午
9346	    if (w.isFastForwardingTime()) return;
9347	    if (!w.clock.isDay || t >= 0.5) return;                       // 仅上午（Main.time<27000）
9348	    // 概率吃 dayRate（Main.cs:64484-64491）：num6=max(dayRate,1)、num7=(27000/num6)×4，
9349	    // rand.Next(num7)==0 → P = dayRate/108000（旅程倍率/全员睡×5 时到访同倍提速）
9350	    const travelDr = Math.max(1, w.dayRate());
9351	    if (Math.random() >= travelDr / (27000 * 4)) return;

9359	    const hy = anchor.home ? anchor.home.y : Math.floor(anchor.cy / TILE);

9364	    let ay = Math.max(4, hy - 20);

9369	    this.entities.add(npc, 'npcs');

9371	    this.newText(Lang.text('Announcement.HasArrived', Lang.npcName(368) ?? ''), 50, 125, 255);
9372	  }

9	// ---- ① 物品 value:SetDefaults1-5 的 `case N:` 块内首个 `value = D;`(break/return 前最贴近的) ----
10	const values = {};
11	{
12	  const body = itemSrc.slice(itemSrc.indexOf('public void SetDefaults1'), itemSrc.indexOf('public void SetDefaults(int Type'));
13	  const caseRe = /\t\tcase (\d+):\n/g;
14	  const marks = [];
15	  let m;

18	    const end = i + 1 < marks.length ? marks[i + 1].at : marks[i].at + 4000;
19	    const seg = body.slice(marks[i].at, end);
20	    const v = seg.match(/value = (\d+)[fL]?;/);
21	    if (v) { values[marks[i].id] = Number(v[1]); continue; }
22	    // buyPrice(plat, gold, silver, copper) 左锚缺省:值 = Σ arg[i]·100^(3-i)
23	    const bp = seg.match(/value = buyPrice\(([\d\s,]+)\)/);
24	    if (bp) {
25	      const args = bp[1].split(',').map((s) => Number(s.trim()));
26	      values[marks[i].id] = args.reduce((a, x) => a * 100 + x, 0) * 100 ** (4 - args.length);
27	    }
28	  }
29	}
30	
31	// ---- ② 商店表:Chest.SetupShop 顶层 case 块(嵌套 switch 的 case 按深度排除) ----

33	const SHOP_SLOT = {
34	  17: 1, 19: 2, 20: 3, 38: 4, 54: 5, 107: 6, 108: 7, 124: 8, 142: 9, 160: 10,
35	  178: 11, 207: 12, 208: 13, 209: 14, 227: 15, 228: 16, 229: 17, 353: 18,
36	  453: 20, 588: 22, 633: 23, 663: 24,
37	};
38	function parseGate(text) {
39	  if (!text) return null;
40	  const has = (s) => text.includes(s);
41	  // 未实现的门直接整体跳过（如 dayTime && IsItAHappyWindyDay = 起风日限定商品）
42	  if (has('IsItAHappyWindyDay') || has('IsItAHappyRainyDay')) return 'skip';
43	  // remix/纪念种世界分支：正向 Main.remixWorld 合取 → 普通世界恒假 → skip；
44	  // !Main.remixWorld 合取 → 普通世界恒真 → 抹成 true 不贡献条件；
45	  // 纪念种旗（tenthAnniversaryWorld/getGoodWorld/infectedSeed）→ 恒 false。
46	  // 抹除后若整条只剩 true/false 字面量（如 `!remix || (纪念 && !getGood)`）
47	  // → 普通世界恒真，返回 null（无门）；仍含其它未映射内容 → 维持 skip
48	  if (/(^|[^!\w.])Main\.remixWorld/.test(text)) return 'skip';
49	  let t = text
50	    .replace(/!Main\.remixWorld/g, 'true')
51	    .replace(/Main\.tenthAnniversaryWorld|Main\.getGoodWorld|Main\.infectedSeed/g, 'false');
52	  const onlyLiterals = !t.replace(/true|false/g, '').replace(/[!|&()\s]/g, '');
53	  if (onlyLiterals) return t.includes('true') ? null : 'skip';
54	  // 复合门：收集全部可映射条件，按固定优先序去重后以 '&&' 连接（Chest.SetupShop 的
55	  // 嵌套 if 语义 = 外层门 && 内层门同时成立才上架，如 hardMode && ZoneGraveyard）
56	  const conds = [];
57	  const push = (c) => { if (!conds.includes(c)) conds.push(c); };
58	  if (has('hardMode')) push('hardMode');
59	  if (has('downedMechBossAny')) push('downedMechBossAny');
60	  // NPC.downedPlantBoss（世纪之花商店门：爆破专家地雷 937 / 松露自动锤 1551 /
61	  // 巫医 Tiki 套·俾格米项链·叶翼 / 画家回声涂层 5344 等，Chest.cs 多处）
62	  if (has('downedPlantBoss')) push('downedPlantBoss');
63	  if (has('downedPirates')) push('downedPirates');
64	  if (has('downedBoss3')) push('downedBoss3');
65	  if (has('downedBoss2')) push('downedBoss2');
66	  if (has('downedBoss1')) push('downedBoss1');
67	  if (has('moonPhase')) push('moonPhase');
68	  if (/!Main\.dayTime|!\s*Main\.dayTime/.test(text)) push('night');
69	  else if (has('dayTime')) push('day');
70	  if (has('ZoneSnow')) push('zoneSnow');
71	  if (has('ZoneJungle')) push('zoneJungle');
72	  if (has('ZoneGraveyard')) push('zoneGraveyard');
73	  if (has('ZoneMeteor')) push('zoneMeteor');
74	  if (has('UnderworldHeight')) push('zoneUnderworld');
75	  if (has('crimson')) push('crimson');
76	  if (has('xMas') || has('halloween')) push('xMas');
77	  if (has('bloodMoon')) push('bloodMoon');
78	  if (has('eclipse')) push('eclipse');
79	  if (has('PartyIsUp') || has('birthdayParty')) push('party');
80	  if (has('LanternNight.LanternsUp')) push('lanternNight');
81	  return conds.length ? conds.join('&&') : 'skip';
82	}
83	
84	const shops = {};
85	{
86	  const start = chestSrc.indexOf('public void SetupShop(int type)');
87	  const body = chestSrc.slice(start);
88	  // 先按括号深度找出顶层 case(depth==2:方法体1 + switch体2)
89	  const lines = body.split('\n');
90	  let depth = 0;
91	  const marks = [];
92	  let inMethod = false;
93	  for (let li = 0; li < lines.length; li++) {
94	    const line = lines[li];
95	    if (!inMethod && line.includes('switch (type)')) { inMethod = true; depth = 1; continue; }
96	    if (!inMethod) continue;
97	    const cm = line.match(/^\t\tcase (\d+):$/);
98	    if (cm && depth === 2) marks.push({ slot: Number(cm[1]), at: li });
99	    if (line.includes('\n\t}\n') && line.trim() === '}' && depth === 1) break; // 方法结束
100	    for (const ch of line) {
101	      if (ch === '{') depth++;
102	      else if (ch === '}') depth--;
103	    }
104	    if (inMethod && depth <= 1 && marks.length) break; // switch 结束
105	  }
106	  // 逐 case 段解析(行号切片)
107	  for (let i = 0; i < marks.length; i++) {
108	    const li0 = marks[i].at + 1;
109	    const li1 = i + 1 < marks.length ? marks[i + 1].at : lines.length;
110	    const stock = [];
111	    let gateStack = [];
112	    let d = 2; // case 体起始深度
113	    for (let li = li0; li < li1; li++) {
114	      const line = lines[li];
115	      const ifM = line.match(/if \((.+)\)/);
116	      if (ifM) gateStack.push({ cond: parseGate(ifM[1]), depth: d });
117	      const defM = line.match(/array\[num\+?\+?\]\.SetDefaults\((\d+)\)/);
118	      if (defM) {
119	        // 全部未闭合门的条件 AND（原版嵌套 if 语义）：任一门 'skip' → 整条丢弃
120	        const open = gateStack.filter((g) => g.depth <= d).map((g) => g.cond).filter(Boolean);
121	        stock.push({ id: Number(defM[1]), cond: open.length ? open.join('&&') : null });
122	      }
123	      for (const ch of line) {
124	        if (ch === '{') d++;
125	        else if (ch === '}') { d--; while (gateStack.length && gateStack[gateStack.length - 1].depth >= d) gateStack.pop(); }
126	      }
127	    }
128	    shops[marks[i].slot] = stock;
129	  }
130	}
131	
132	// ---- 输出:按 NPC id 组织,附价格 ----
133	const out = {};
134	for (const [npcId, slot] of Object.entries(SHOP_SLOT)) {
135	  // 任一开门含 'skip'（未映射条件，如 HasItem/随机掷骰）→ 整条丢弃（AND 语义）；
136	  // cond=null（无开门）= 恒上架，保留
137	  const stock = (shops[slot] ?? []).filter((s) => s.cond === null || !s.cond.split('&&').includes('skip'));
138	  out[npcId] = stock.map((s) => ({ id: s.id, price: values[s.id] ?? null, cond: s.cond }));
139	}
140	writeFileSync('src/data/vanilla-shopstock.json', JSON.stringify({ values, shops: out }));
141	const total = Object.values(out).reduce((a, s) => a + s.length, 0);

```json
{
 "command": "node --input-type=module -e \"\nimport { readFileSync } from 'fs';\nconst chestSrc = readFileSync('~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/Terraria/Chest.cs','utf8');\nconst json = JSON.parse(readFileSync('~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/data/vanilla-shopstock.json','utf8'));\nconst SHOP_SLOT = {17:1,19:2,20:3,38:4,54:5,107:6,108:7,124:8,142:9,160:10,178:11,207:12,208:13,209:14,227:15,228:16,229:17,353:18,368:19,453:20,550:21,588:22,633:23,663:24};\nconst start = chestSrc.indexOf('public void SetupShop(int type)');\nconst body = chestSrc.slice(start);\nconst lines = body.split('\\n');\nlet depth=0, inMethod=false; const marks=[];\nfor (let li=0; li<lines.length; li++){\n  const line=lines[li];\n  if(!inMethod && line.includes('switch (type)')){inMethod=true;depth=1;continue;}\n  if(!inMethod) continue;\n  const cm=line.match(/^\\t\\tcase (\\d+):\\$/);\n  if(cm && depth===2) marks.push({slot:Number(cm[1]),at:li});\n  for(const ch of line){ if(ch==='{')depth++; else if(ch==='}')depth--; }\n  if(inMethod && depth<=1 && marks.length) break;\n}\n// parse per case with raw gates\nconst shops={};\nfor(let i=0;i<marks.length;i++){\n  const li0=marks[i].at+1, li1=i+1<marks.length?marks[i+1].at:lines.length;\n  const stock=[]; let gates=[]; let d=2;\n  for(let li=li0;li<li1;li++){\n    const line=lines[li];\n    const ifM=line.match(/if \\((.+)\\)/);\n    if(ifM) gates.push({cond:ifM[1],depth:d});\n    const defM=line.match(/array\\[num\\+?\\+?\\]\\.SetDefaults\\((\\d+)\\)/);\n    if(defM){\n      // look ahead up to 4 lines for custom price / currency\n      let cp=null,cur=null;\n      for(let k=1;k<=4;k++){\n        const nl=(lines[li+k]||'');\n        const cpm=nl.match(/shopCustomPrice = (?:Item\\.buyPrice\\(([^)]*)\\)|(\\d+))/);\n        if(cpm) cp = cpm[1]?('buy('+cpm[1]+')'):cpm[2];\n        const cm2=nl.match(/shopSpecialCurrency = (\\w+[\\w.]*)/);\n        if(cm2) cur=cm2[1];\n        if(nl.includes('num++')&&cp===null&&cur===null) break;\n        if(nl.includes('break')) break;\n      }\n      const open=gates.filter(g=>g.depth<=d).map(g=>g.cond);\n      stock.push({id:Number(defM[1]),gates:open,cp,cur});\n    }\n    for(const ch of line){ if(ch==='{')d++; else if(ch==='}'){d--; while(gates.length&&gates[gates.length-1].depth>=d)gates.pop();} }\n  }\n  shops[marks[i].slot]=stock;\n}\n// report\nconst nameOf={};\nfor(const [npcId,slot] of Object.entries(SHOP_SLOT)){\n  const src=shops[slot]||[];\n  const impl=json.shops[npcId]||[];\n  const implIds=new Set(impl.map(s=>s.id));\n  const missing=src.filter(s=>!implIds.has(s.id));\n  const custom=src.filter(s=>s.cp||s.cur);\n  console.log('=== npc',npcId,'slot',slot,'| src items:',src.length,'impl:',impl.length,'| dropped:',missing.length,'| customPrice:',custom.length);\n  for(const m of missing) console.log('   DROP id',m.id,'gates:',JSON.stringify(m.gates));\n  for(const c of custom) console.log('   CUST id',c.id,'cp',c.cp,'cur',c.cur);\n}\nconsole.log('slots present in chest:',Object.keys(shops).join(','));\n\" 2>&1 | head -200",
 "description": "Full diff of source SetupShop vs implementation JSON"
}

const marks=[];\nfor (let li=0; li<lines.length; li++){\n  const line=lines[li];\n  if(!inMethod && line.includes('switch (type)')){inMethod=true;depth=1;continue;}\n  if(!inMethod) continue;\n  const cm=line.match(/^\\t\\tcase (\\d+):\\$/);\n  if(cm && depth===2) marks.push({slot:Number(cm[1]),at:li});\n  for(const ch of line){ if(ch==='{')depth++; else if(ch==='}')depth--; }\n  if(inMethod && depth<=1 && marks.length) break;\n}\nconst shops={};\nfor(let i=0;i<marks.length;i++){\n  const li0=marks[i].at+1, li1=i+1<marks.length?marks[i+1].at:lines.length;\n  const stock=[]; let gates=[]; let d=2;\n  for(let li=li0;li<li1;li++){\n    const line=lines[li];\n    const ifM=line.match(/if \\((.+)\\)/);\n    if(ifM) gates.push({cond:ifM[1],depth:d});\n    const defM=line.match(/array\\[num\\+?\\+?\\]\\.SetDefaults\\((\\d+)\\)/);\n    if(defM){ const open=gates.filter(g=>g.depth<=d).map(g=>g.cond); const impl=json.shops[npcId]||[];\n  const implIds=new Set(impl.map(s=>s.id));\n  const missing=src.filter(s=>!implIds.has(s.id));\n  console.log('=== npc',npcId,'src',src.length,'impl',impl.length);\n  for(const m of missing) console.log('   DROP',m.id,JSON.stringify(m.gates));\n}\nconsole.log('slots in chest:',Object.keys(shops).join(','));\nconsole.log('npc keys in json:',Object.keys(json.shops).join(','));\n// line ranges per slot\nfor(const m of marks) console.log('slot',m.slot,'line',m.at+1);\n\" 2>&1 | tail -50",

	}

	private float GetSkeletonMerchantPrices(NPC npc)
	{
		float num = 1f;
		if (Main.moonPhase == 1 || Main.moonPhase == 7)
		{
			num = 1.1f;
		}
		if (Main.moonPhase == 2 || Main.moonPhase == 6)
		{
			num = 1.2f;
		}
		if (Main.moonPhase == 3 || Main.moonPhase == 5)
		{
			num = 1.3f;
		}
		if (Main.moonPhase == 4)
		{
			num = 1.4f;
		}
		if (Main.dayTime)
		{
			num += 0.1f;
		}
		return num;
	}

	private float GetTravelingMerchantPrices(NPC npc)
	{
		Vector2 value = npc.Center / 16f;
		Vector2 value2 = new Vector2(Main.spawnTileX, Main.spawnTileY);
		float num = Vector2.Distance(value, value2) / (float)(Main.maxTilesX / 2);
		num = 1.5f - num;
		return (2f + num) / 3f;
	}

	private void ProcessMood(Player player, NPC npc)
	{
		_currentHappiness = "";
		_currentPriceAdjustment = 1f;
		if (npc.loveStruck)
		{
			_currentPriceAdjustment *= 0.9f;
		}
		if (Main.remixWorld || npc.type == 368 || npc.type == 453 || NPCID.Sets.IsTownPet[npc.type] || IsNotReallyTownNPC(npc))
		{
			return;
		}
		if (RuinMoodIfHomeless(npc))
		{
			_currentPriceAdjustment = 1000f;
		}
		else if (IsFarFromHome(npc))
		{
			_currentPriceAdjustment = 1000f;
		}
		if (IsPlayerInEvilBiomes(player))
		{
			_currentPriceAdjustment = 1000f;
		}
		int npcsWithinHouse;
		int npcsWithinVillage;
		List<NPC> nearbyResidentNPCs = GetNearbyResidentNPCs(npc, out npcsWithinHouse, out npcsWithinVillage);
		bool flag = true;
		float num = 1.05f;
		if (npc.type == 663)
		{
			flag = false;
			num = 1f;
			if (npcsWithinHouse < 2 && npcsWithinVillage < 2)
			{
				AddHappinessReportText("HateLonely");
				_currentPriceAdjustment = 1000f;
			}
		}
		if (true && npcsWithinHouse > 3)
		{
			for (int i = 3; i < npcsWithinHouse; i++)
			{
				_currentPriceAdjustment *= num;
			}
			if (npcsWithinHouse > 6)
			{
				AddHappinessReportText("HateCrowded");
			}
			else
			{
				AddHappinessReportText("DislikeCrowded");
			}
		}
		if (flag && npcsWithinHouse <= 2 && npcsWithinVillage < 4)
		{
			AddHappinessReportText("LoveSpace");
			_currentPriceAdjustment *= 0.95f;
		}
		bool[] array = new bool[NPCID.Count];
		foreach (NPC item in nearbyResidentNPCs)
		{
			array[item.type] = true;
		}
		HelperInfo info = new HelperInfo
		{
			player = player,
			npc = npc,
			NearbyNPCs = nearbyResidentNPCs,
			nearbyNPCsByType = array
		};
		foreach (IShopPersonalityTrait shopModifier in _database.GetByNPCID(npc.type).ShopModifiers)
		{
			shopModifier.ModifyShopPrice(info, this);
		}
		new AllPersonalitiesModifier().ModifyShopPrice(info, this);
		if (_currentHappiness == "")
		{
			AddHappinessReportText("Content");
		}
		_currentPriceAdjustment = LimitAndRoundMultiplier(_currentPriceAdjustment);
	}

## 👤 User(sidechain) · 2026-08-13T01:53:48.718Z

**📎 ToolResult**

```
export class NpcShop {
  private panel: HTMLElement | null = null;
  private escHandler: ((e: KeyboardEvent) => void) | null = null;

  constructor(private parent: HTMLElement, private onBuy: (vanillaId: number) => void, private onClose: () => void) {}

  open(title: string, items: ShopEntry[], copper: number) {
    this.close();
    const panel = document.createElement('div');
    panel.className = 'sw-npc-shop';
    const rows = items.map((it) => `
      <div class="sw-shop-item${copper < it.price ? ' poor' : ''}" data-id="${it.vanillaId}" data-ok="${copper >= it.price ? 1 : 0}">
        ${it.iconUrl ? `<img src="${it.iconUrl}">` : '<span style="width:26px;height:26px;display:inline-block"></span>'}
        <span class="nm">${it.name}</span><span class="pr">${formatCopper(it.price)}</span>
      </div>`).join('');
    panel.innerHTML = `
      <div class="sw-shop-title">${title}</div>
      <div class="sw-shop-coins">${formatCopper(copper)}</div>
      <div class="sw-shop-list">${rows}</div>
      <div class="sw-shop-foot"><button class="sw-set-back" style="background:#3a4680;color:#e8e8f4;border:1px solid #7d92d6;border-radius:4px;padding:5px 16px;cursor:pointer;font-family:inherit;">${Lang.inter(52)}</button></div>`;
    panel.querySelectorAll('.sw-shop-item').forEach((el) => {
      el.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if ((el as HTMLElement).dataset.ok !== '1') return;
        this.onBuy(Number((el as HTMLElement).dataset.id));
      });
    });
    (panel.querySelector('.sw-set-back') as HTMLElement).addEventListener('click', () => this.onClose());
    this.parent.appendChild(panel);
    this.panel = panel;
    this.escHandler = (e: KeyboardEvent) => { if (e.code === 'Escape') this.onClose(); };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', this.escHandler);
  }

  close() {
    if (this.escHandler) window.removeEventListener('keydown', this.escHandler);
    this.escHandler = null;
    this.panel?.remove();
    this.panel = null;
  }

  get isOpen(): boolean { return this.panel !== null; }
}

```

      // 骷髅商(cs:95948-95959):常规 356-363 + SkeletonMerchantChatter 池
      const r = rN(9); // 8 常规 + Chatter 池 1 条
      if (r >= 8) return Lang.chatter('SkeletonMerchantChatter') ?? dialog(356);
      return dialog(356 + r);
    }
    // 其余类型(旅行商人368/骷髅商453/钓鱼娃369 等非房屋 NPC 未注册,不可达)
    return '……';
  }

  /** LegacyDialog 命名占位符替换（原版 LocalizedText 显示期 Format 语义）：
   *  {PlayerName}=主角名；{Guide} 等 = 对应城镇 NPC 当前名（在场实例带随机名，否则类型名）；
   *  {WorldEvilStone}=黑檀石/猩红石（按世界邪恶） */
  private formatDialogTags(s: string): string {
    const townName = (key: string): string => {
      const inst = this.entities.npcs.find(
        (n) => n instanceof TownNPC && !n.dead && (n as TownNPC).npcKey === key) as TownNPC | undefined;
      const base = Lang.npcName(TOWN_NPC_IDS[key]?.id ?? 0) ?? key;
      return inst?.givenName ? Lang.text('Game.NPCTitle', inst.givenName, base) : base;
    };
    return s
      .replace(/\{PlayerName\}/g, this.player.appearance?.name || '泰拉瑞亚')
      .replace(/\{Guide\}/g, townName('guide'))
      .replace(/\{Nurse\}/g, townName('nurse'))
      .replace(/\{Dryad\}/g, townName('dryad'))
      .replace(/\{Demolitionist\}/g, townName('demolitionist'))
      .replace(/\{ArmsDealer\}/g, townName('arms_dealer'))
      .replace(/\{Merchant\}/g, townName('merchant'))
      .replace(/\{Bartender\}/g, Lang.npcName(550) ?? '酒馆老板') // 酒馆老板未实装,恒用类型名
      .replace(/\{WorldEvilStone\}/g,
        Lang.itemName(this.world.crimson ? 836 : 61)   // ItemID.cs:3211/1661：Crimstone/Ebonstone Block
        ?? (this.world.crimson ? '猩红石' : '黑檀石'));
  }

  /** 对话框按钮(SetTalkNPC 后 UI 回调) */
  npcDialogButton(id: NpcButtonId): void {
    const npc = this.dialogNpc;
    if (id === 'close' || !npc) { this.closeNpcDialog(); return; }
    if (id === 'shop') { this.openNpcShop(); return; }
    if (id === 'heal') { this.nurseHeal(); return; }
    if (id === 'collect' && npc.npcKey === 'tax_collector') { this.taxCollect(); return; }
    if (id === 'reforge' && npc.npcKey === 'goblin_tinkerer') {
      // 原版 NPCChatText_TinkererReforge（Main.cs:39431）：关对话进重铸界面
      this.closeNpcDialog();
      this.cb.onReforgeOpen?.();
      return;
    }
    if (id === 'curse' && npc.npcKey === 'old_man') {
      if (this.boss) { this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.BossActive')); return; }
      // 原版:释放诅咒时老人消失,击败骷髅王后转为裁缝
      npc.dead = true;
      this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.NPC.OldManScream'));
      this.closeNpcDialog();
      this.summonBossAtTx('skeletron_head', Math.floor(npc.cx / TILE));
      return;
    }
    if (id === 'quest' && npc.vanillaId === 369) { this.anglerQuestTurnIn(); return; }
  }

  // ================= 渔夫任务链（Main.anglerQuest* + Player.GetAnglerReward）=================

  /** 当日任务鱼 id（Main.anglerQuestItemNetIDs[Main.anglerQuest]，Main.cs:20091 同式） */
  anglerQuestItemId(): number { return ANGLER_QUEST_ITEMS[this.world.anglerQuest] ?? 0; }

  /** Main.AnglerQuestSwap（Main.cs:3799-3947）：清今日交付名单/完成旗 + 按世界门重掷。
   *  调用点 = 黎明（UpdateTime_StartDay :64852）+ 新世界（WorldGen.cs:22655）。
   *  掷骰注入按「种子 ^ 天数」播种的独立 UnifiedRandom → 同种子同天数同任务鱼

```

86:The **Tavernkeep** is a unique [NPC](NPC "NPC") vendor who only accepts [Defender Medals](Defender_Medal.md "Defender Medal") as currency for most items instead of [coins](Coins.md "Coins"). He will move in once the following criteria have been met: 
87-
88-  * There is an available vacant [house](House.md "House").
89-  * Rescuing him as the **Unconscious Man** , who can be encountered in any [layer](Layer "Layer") once the [Eater of Worlds](Eater_of_Worlds.md "Eater of Worlds") or [Brain of Cthulhu](Brain_of_Cthulhu.md "Brain of Cthulhu") has been defeated. Interacting with him in this state with [`⚷ Open / Activate`](Game_controls.md "Game controls") will wake him. If the Unconscious Man is killed, he will still be able to respawn afterwards; if the Tavernkeep NPC is killed, he will move in again after some time as long as there are vacant [houses](House.md "House").

193:  * Though the Party Girl is mostly seen as a source of novelty and decorative items, a few of her items are fairly useful: 
194-    * The [Bubbles](Bubble.md "Bubble") that she sells have a variety of practical uses, such as holding honey and water in midair to provide [health regeneration](Health_regeneration.md "Health regeneration") and activate the [Cute Fishron](Shrimpy_Truffle.md "Shrimpy Truffle")'s speed boost, respectively.
195-    * [Pigronatas](Pigronata.md "Pigronata") drop [Party Bullets](Party_Bullet.md "Party Bullet"), which have better stats than [Silver Bullets](Silver_Bullet.md "Silver Bullet").
196-    * The [Slice of Cake](Slice_of_Cake.md "Slice of Cake") she offers to players provides a buff that is useful for building.
--
349:  * The Party Girl and the [Princess](Princess.md "Princess") are the only female [NPCs](NPCs.md "NPCs") who do not appear annoyed or rude during a [Blood Moon](Blood_Moon.md "Blood Moon"). The Party Girl is the only female NPC to not make any comment about the Blood Moon at all.
350-  * [Redigit](Redigit.md "Redigit") and [Cenx](Cenx.md "Cenx") have stated that the Party Girl was inspired by the character [Pinkie Pie](https://mlp.fandom.com/wiki/Pinkie_Pie) from the Canadian-American children's animated fantasy television series _[My Little Pony: Friendship Is Magic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/en:My_Little_Pony:_Friendship_Is_Magic "wikipedia:en:My Little Pony: Friendship Is Magic")_.[3][4]
351-    * The names Sparkle and Trixy are likely references to the characters [Princess Twilight Sparkle](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/en:Princess_Twilight_Sparkle "wikipedia:en:Princess Twilight Sparkle") and [Trixie](https://mlp.fandom.com/wiki/Trixie) from the same series.
352-    * The Party Girl's Shimmered form resembles [Rainbow Dash](https://mlp.fandom.com/wiki/Rainbow_Dash) from the same series.

### 最需修复 TOP 8

1. **价格兜底 100 铜**（#2）：158 条 price:null→`Game.ts:9198`。修 extract-shops.mjs 支持 `SetShopValues`/`sellPrice`（各加两行正则）重跑即可，全店价格崩坏一次性收敛。公主店 1 银币卖 100 金币套装是当前最荒谬的可见 bug。
2. **传送塔段**（#3）：Chest.cs:3448-3518 通用尾段 1:1 移植到 shopStockFor 末尾（9 塔×21 店，群系判定 repo 已有 scene zones）。
3. **酒馆老板**（#1）：SHOP_SLOT 补 `550:21` + `shopSpecialCurrency`/`shopCustomPrice` 提取 + 勋章货币轨道（若 NPC 暂不实装至少登记）。
4. **提取器三缺陷**（#4a/4b/4c）：OR→AND、`!hardMode` 取反、else 互斥——都是 parseGate 一处小改，收益是军火商/树妖/蒸汽/电子人/派对女孩/裁缝六家店的条件语义全部归位。
5. **手写表错挂**（#7）+ **重复上架**（#8/#9）：4744→裁缝、4743→派对女孩；动物学家先剔 8 件套装再按相位补（照抄 :9222 树妖盆栽的剔除法）；删 `Game.ts:9202` 的 4702 手工补。
6. **油漆工第二商店**（#6）：Decor case 25 约 20 件 + `Shop(227,25)` 第二按钮。
7. **圣诞老人/油漆工深漆 for 循环**（#5/#6）：提取器正则补 `SetDefaults(变量)` 的循环展开。
8. **售出路径**（#12）：商店面板加 2 个出售槽 + `isAShopItem` 当次全额回购（Player.cs:34923-34925）。

另：任务前提两处需更正——①幸福度**已实装**（vanillaHappiness.ts 全表），仅缺 loveStruck×0.9；②"稀有概率售卖/哥布林月相血月换货"在 1.4.5.6 城镇商店**不存在**（SetupShop 全段零随机，源码+wiki 双证实），概率货仅旅行商一家。
